<h2 id="sources">Sources and coverage</h2>
<p>Trades: FINRA TRACE via the public lookup, collected every 15 minutes for the core watch and hourly for the benchmark tier; the stored tape begins 7 October 2026. Marks: SEC N-PORT bulk filings. A fund mark is a period-end valuation, not a trade price, and filings lag the period end by about 60 days. Yields are yield-to-worst, semiannual 30/360; spreads are against the interpolated US Treasury curve. Benchmark bonds carry no fund marks. Quantities the window cannot support are stated as absent, not estimated.</p>
